00002354                    ; R9  s=speed

00002370                    ; R11=address of current word

0000285C          
0000285C                    ;
0000285C                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
0000285C                    ;
0000285C                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
0000285C                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
0000285C                    ;
0000285C                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
0000285C                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
0000285C                    ;
0000285C          
0000285C          
0000285C          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
0000285C E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
00002860 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
00002864 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
00002868          
00002868          
00002868 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
0000286C E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
00002870 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
00002874          
00002874          
00002874 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
00002878 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
0000287C E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
00002880          .realvu_channelloop 
00002880 EBFFFFFE           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
00002884 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
00002888 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
0000288C E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
00002890 E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
00002894 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
00002898          
00002898          
00002898 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
0000289C E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
000028A0 E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
000028A4 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
000028A8 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
000028AC 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
000028B0 E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
000028B4 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
000028B8          
000028B8          
000028B8 E589302C           STR     R3,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;store new heights for channels 5-8
000028BC E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000028C0          
000028C0          
000028C0          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
000028C0 E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
000028C4 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000028C8 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000028CC C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000028D0 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000028D4 CAFFFFFE           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000028D8          
000028D8          
000028D8 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000028DC 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000028E0 DAFFFFFE           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
000028E4          
000028E4          
000028E4          .realvu_chanrunning 
000028E4 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
000028E8 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000028EC 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000028F0 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
000028F4          
000028F4          
000028F4 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
000028F8 AAFFFFFE           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....
000028FC          
000028FC          
000028FC E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002900 B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002904 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002908 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
0000290C          
0000290C          
0000290C E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002910 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002914          
00002914          
00002914                    OPT opt%
00002914 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002918 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
0000291C                    FNlong_adr("  ",10,loglintab)
0000291C E3A0B000           MOV     R11,#0
00002920 E3A0C080           MOV     R12,#128                ;get average real vu height from 128 bytes of DMA data
00002924          
00002924          
00002924          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002924 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
00002928 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
0000292C C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
00002930 C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
00002934 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
00002938 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
0000293C A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
00002940 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
00002944          
00002944          
00002944 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
00002948 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
00002950          
00002950          
00002950                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
00002950                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
00002950          
00002950          
00002950 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
00002954 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
00002958 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
0000295C E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
00002960 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
00002964 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
00002968 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
0000296C          
0000296C          
0000296C          .realvu_blankchannel 
0000296C E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
00002970 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}        ;exit with R0=vu height for this channel
00002974          
00002974          
00002974                    ;
00002974                    ; SWI QTM_DMABuffer and fake DMA Buffer creation routine for 16bit sound RiscPCs
00002974                    ;
00002974          
00002974          
00002974          .swi_qtmdmabuffer 
00002974 E59C0078           LDR     R0,[R12,#fake8bitDMA]
00002978 E3300000           TEQ     R0,#0
0000297C 059C00BC           LDREQ   R0,[R12,#DMABuffer]
00002980 01A0F00E           MOVEQ   PC,R14
00002984          
00002984          
00002984 E92D4000           STMFD   R13!,{R14}
00002988 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope               ;update fake DMA block for 16bit-sound RiscPCs
0000298C E8BD8000           LDMFD   R13!,{PC}
00002990          
00002990          
00002990          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
00002990 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!
00002994 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
00002998 E599C078           LDR     R12,[R9,#fake8bitDMA]           ;R12=fake DMA address
0000299C          
0000299C          
0000299C E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000029A0 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000029A4 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
000029A8 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
000029AC E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
000029B0          
000029B0          
000029B0 E1A0600B           MOV     R6,R11
000029B4          .calcscope_channelloop 
000029B4 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope_chanctrl
000029B8          
000029B8          
000029B8 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
000029BC E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
000029C0 E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
000029C4 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop
000029C8 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000029CC          
000029CC          
000029CC          .calcscope_chanctrl 
000029CC E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}       ;
000029D0 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000029D4 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000029D8 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000029DC C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000029E0 CAFFFFFE           BGT     calcscope_blankchannel  ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000029E4          
000029E4          
000029E4 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000029E8 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000029EC DAFFFFFE           BLE     calcscope_blankchannel
000029F0          
000029F0          
000029F0          .calcscope_chanrunning 
000029F0 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
000029F4 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000029F8 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000029FC E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002A00          
00002A00          
00002A00 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002A04 AAFFFFFE           BGE     calcscope_blankchannel  ;go to clear the channel...
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002A0C B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002A10 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002A14 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch

00002A1C B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002A20          
00002A20          
00002A20          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A20 E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002A24 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume
00002A28 43A06000           MOVMI   R6,#0                   ;reset to 0 if vol too low
00002A2C E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;store in fake DMA block

00002A34 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start
00002A38          
00002A38          
00002A38 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;check fake DMA end...
00002A3C BAFFFFF7           BLT     calcscope_sampleloop    ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002A40 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A44          
00002A44          
00002A44          .calcscope_blankchannel 
00002A44 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;
00002A48          .calcscope_clear ;
00002A48 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A4C E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002A50 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A54 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A58 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A5C B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A60 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A64 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002A68 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002A6C E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit

00003668                    .CIAhandler                    ;r12 = song_data on entry
00003668 E92D4007           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,R14}       ; * stack r0,r1,r14 and for v1.43se RasterMan, also store r2
0000366C E89C0003           LDMIA   R12,{R0-R1}            ;r0=CIA delay, r1=CIA counter
00003670 E59C2420           LDR     R2,[R12,#rm_CIAdec]    ; * v1.43se RasterMan
00003674                    
00003674          
00003674 E0511002           SUBS    R1,R1,R2               ; * take 1st lump (50Hz) of accurate time off CIA counter
00003678 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
0000367C E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
00003680 DBFFFFFE           BLLE    ControlSong            ; * as we're now at 50Hz (but code expects 100Hz) so call twice!
00003684                    
00003684          
00003684 E0511002           SUBS    R1,R1,R2               ; * take 2nd lump (50Hz) of accurate time off CIA counter
00003688 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
0000368C E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
00003690 E8BD4007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,R14}       ; * reload r0,r1,r14 and r2
00003694 C1A0F00E           MOVGT   PC,R14                 ;if CIA counter didn't run out, then exit

00003698          .ControlSong 
00003698 E92D4FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R11,R14}
0000369C E5DC0009           LDRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;load speed counter

000036A4 D5DC0008           LDRLEB  R0,[R12,#speed]         ;if counter<=0 then reload it with the speed
000036A8 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;store counter
000036AC CAFFFFFE           BGT     doFX                    ;if counter didn't get to 0, then go and do the effects...
000036B0

00003FA0 E5C10000           STRB    R0,[R1]
00003FA4 E8BD8007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,PC}
00003FA8                    ; - this very rare effect inverts actual sample data (in memory),
00003FA8                    ;   and is the only effect NOT included in QTM<v1.48. Although I
00003FA8                    ;   coded the routine, I never found a song which uses it, until
00003FA8                    ;   4mat provided a test track ;) thanks! Now fully implememnted.
00003FA8                    ;                                                             SH
00003FA8          .funktable

0000419C E3110C01           TST     R1,#1<<8

000041A4 E3110C02           TST     R1,#2<<8

000041C0 A08003A3           ADDGE   R0,R0,R3,LSR#7

00004270          .do_tremolo ; ***** 07 - tremolo *****
00004270 E5DB000F           LDRB    R0,[R11,#currentvol]
00004274 E59B1028           LDR     R1,[R11,#tremolo_speed] ;0-3 depth, 4-7 speed, 8-9 wave, 10=no reset, 26-30 pos, 31 sign
00004278 E201331F           AND     R3,R1,#&1F<<26          ;=&7C<<24 ;get pos in bits 26-30 (bit 31 is sign, so clear it)
0000427C E3110C03           TST     R1,#%11<<8
00004280                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R12,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &FF00
00004280                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R4,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &00FF
00004280 024F4038           ADREQ   R4,vibratotable_new
00004284 07D43D23           LDREQB  R3,[R4,R3,LSR#26]       ;not ASR, as b31 just used to flag add/sub value
00004288 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     trem_set                ;PT prioritises 0/4 (sine)
0000428C          
0000428C          
0000428C E3110C01           TST R1,#%01<<8                  ;...then 1/5 (sawtooth)
00004290 1AFFFFFE           BNE trem_rampdown
00004294 E3A030FF           MOV R3,#255 ;1 seems to work with 4mat test track  ;...then 2/6 (sqr, and 3/7 treated as 2/6)
00004298          .trem_set 
00004298 E201400F           AND     R4,R1,#&0F              ;depth
0000429C E0030394           MUL     R3,R4,R3                ;depth*value
000042A0 E21124FF           ANDS    R2,R1,#&FF000000        ;R2=bits 26-30 pos (+b31 sign) (b24 & b25=0, because pos 26-30)
000042A4 50800323           ADDPL   R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042A8 40500323           SUBMIS  R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042AC 43A00000           MOVMI   R0,#0
000042B0 E3500040           CMP     R0,#64
000042B4 C3A00040           MOVGT   R0,#64
000042B8          
000042B8          
000042B8 E5CA000C           STRB    R0,[R10,#DMA_volume]
000042BC          
000042BC          
000042BC E20110F0           AND     R1,R1,#&F0       ;=speed in bits %11110000
000042C0 E0822B01           ADD     R2,R2,R1,LSL#(24-2) ;add speed to pos (bits 26-30 + b31 sign)
000042C4 E1A02C42           MOV     R2,R2,ASR#24     ;keep sign, move to bits 0-7 (v1.49 fix - vibrato was fixed in 1.46a!)
000042C8 E5CB202B           STRB    R2,[R11,#tremolo_pos]
000042CC EAFFFDD7           B       nextFX

0000549C          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
0000559C                    OPT opt%
0000559C          
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
0000559C                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
0000559C                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
0000559C                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
0000559C                    ;
0000559C          
0000559C          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
0000559C E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
000055A0 E24F2008           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
000055A4          
000055A4                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
000055A4 E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
000055A8 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
000055AC          .shuffleloop 
000055AC E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
000055B0 E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
000055B4 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
000055B8 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
000055BC          
000055BC          
000055BC E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
000055C0 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000055C4          
000055C4          
000055C4 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
000055C8 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
000055CC E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
000055D0 E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055D4 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
000055D8          .skipthisshuffle 
000055D8 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
000055DC E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055E0 E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055E4 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055E8 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
000055EC          
000055EC

0000562C                    ; on entry
0000562C          
0000562C                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
0000562C                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
0000562C                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
0000562C                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
0000562C                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
0000562C                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
0000562C                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
0000562C                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
0000562C                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
0000562C                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
0000562C                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
0000562C                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
0000562C                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
0000562C                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
0000562C                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
0000562C

000067EC                    ; Trial of improved and shorter looped DIV code
000067EC          
000067EC                    ; on entry - R0=A +ve; R1=B +ve >0
000067EC                    ; on exit - R0=A DIV B; R1=A MOD B
000067EC          
000067EC                    ;R0=num
000067EC                    ;R1=den
000067EC                    ;R2=cnt
000067EC                    ;R3=mod
000067EC          
000067EC                    ; seems to equal the unrolled AU div routine when dividing large 32 bit numbers by 512+

000068CC E3A0B000           MOV     R11,#0                  ;clear all vars
000068D0          .continueinit 
000068D0                    ;
000068D0                    ;  Reset song data to default
000068D0                    ;
000068D0 E35B0001           CMP     R11,#1                  ;0 (NE) if a full re-init
000068D4 E3A00802           MOV     R0,#2<<16               ;CIA delay (100th sec takes off 1<<16, so 2<<16 gives 50Hz)
000068D8 E3A01000           MOV     R1,#0                   ;CIA counter
000068DC E3A02006           MOV        R2,#&00000006        ;speed (6), counter, pattern delay }_ bytes in the
000068E0 E382247D           ORR     R2,R2,#&7D000000        ;CIA tempo (&7D = 125)             }  same word
000068E4 13A03000           MOVNE   R3,#0
000068E8 158C307C           STRNE   R3,[R12,#patternlen]    ;if full init, reset pattern length to 0
000068EC 059C300C           LDREQ   R3,[R12,#songaddr]
000068F0 E3A04000           MOV     R4,#0                   ;current event, sequence
000068F4 E3A05000           MOV     R5,#0                   ;last event, pattern, sequence
000068F8 E59C6018           LDR     R6,[R12,#vuflags]       ;don't wipe vu flags/state/gravity
000068FC E3C664FF           BIC     R6,R6,#&FF000000        ;            ...but wipe last byte (songstopflags)
00006900 13A07000           MOVNE   R7,#0                   ;if init, clear break flags/pos & max sequence/pattern
00006904 059C701C           LDREQ   R7,[R12,#breakflags]    ;
00006908 03C770FF           BICEQ   R7,R7,#&000000FF        ;if 'clear song vars', clear break flags/pos, but not
0000690C 03C77CFF           BICEQ   R7,R7,#&0000FF00        ;  max sequence/pattern
00006910          
00006910          
00006910 E88C00FF           STMIA   R12,{R0-R7}             ;reset/blank first 32 bytes
00006914          
00006914          
00006914 128CA030           ADDNE   R10,R12,#8*4+16         ;skip first 32b & effect flags & vu heights - don't reset these!
00006918 13A00000           MOVNE   R0,#0                   ;
0000691C 13A02000           MOVNE   R2,#0                   ;
00006920 13A03000           MOVNE   R3,#0                   ;clear start/pause times
00006924 18AA000F           STMNEIA R10!,{R0-R3}            ;that's the first 64 bytes reset (only if full init)
00006928 128AA004           ADDNE   R10,R10,#4              ;don't overwrite 'workspaceptr'
0000692C 13A00000           MOVNE   R0,#0                   ;song state/type/actual_dma/chans (4+64) - ok to blank actual dma, resets in init_db
00006930 13A01000           MOVNE   R1,#0                   ;song length (8+64)
00006934 13A02000           MOVNE   R2,#0                   ;garbage data (12+64)
00006938 18AA0007           STMNEIA R10!,{R0-R2}            ;(wipe above if full init)

00002354                    ; R9  s=speed

00002370                    ; R11=address of current word
00002370                    ; R10=CIA tempo (32-255)

00002370                    ; R8=time per line = (10*speed/4*cia tempo)*65536

0000285C                    ;
0000285C                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
0000285C                    ;
0000285C                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
0000285C                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
0000285C                    ;
0000285C                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
0000285C                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
0000285C                    ;
0000285C          
0000285C          
0000285C          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
0000285C E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
00002860 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
00002864 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
00002868          
00002868          
00002868 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
0000286C E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
00002870 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
00002874          
00002874          
00002874 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
00002878 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
0000287C E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
00002880          .realvu_channelloop 
00002880 EB00000E           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
00002884 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
00002888 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
0000288C E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
00002890 E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
00002894 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
00002898          
00002898          
00002898 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
0000289C E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
000028A0 E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
000028A4 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
000028A8 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
000028AC 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
000028B0 E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
000028B4 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
000028B8          
000028B8          
000028B8 E589302C           STR     R3,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;store new heights for channels 5-8
000028BC E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000028C0          
000028C0          
000028C0          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
000028C0 E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
000028C4 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000028C8 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000028CC C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000028D0 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000028D4 CA000024           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000028D8          
000028D8          
000028D8 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000028DC 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000028E0 DA000021           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
000028E4          
000028E4          
000028E4          .realvu_chanrunning 
000028E4 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
000028E8 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000028EC 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000028F0 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
000028F4

000028F8 AA00001B           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....

000028FC

0000290C          
0000290C          
0000290C E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002910 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002914

00002914                    OPT opt%

00002924          
00002924          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002924 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
00002928 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
0000292C C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
00002930 C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
00002934 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
00002938 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
0000293C A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
00002940 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
00002944          
00002944          
00002944 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
00002948 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
00002950          
00002950          
00002950                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
00002950                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
00002950          
00002950          
00002950 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
00002954 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
00002958 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
0000295C E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
00002960 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
00002964 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
00002968 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
0000296C

0000296C          .realvu_blankchannel 
0000296C E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0

00002990          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
00002990 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!

000029A0 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000029A4 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
000029A8 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
000029AC E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
000029B0

000029B8          
000029B8          
000029B8 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
000029BC E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
000029C0 E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
000029C4 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop
000029C8 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000029CC          
000029CC          
000029CC          .calcscope_chanctrl 
000029CC E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}       ;
000029D0 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000029D4 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000029D8 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000029DC C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000029E0 CA000017           BGT     calcscope_blankchannel  ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000029E4          
000029E4          
000029E4 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000029E8 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000029EC DA000014           BLE     calcscope_blankchannel
000029F0          
000029F0          
000029F0          .calcscope_chanrunning 
000029F0 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
000029F4 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000029F8 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000029FC E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002A00          
00002A00          
00002A00 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002A04 AA00000E           BGE     calcscope_blankchannel  ;go to clear the channel...
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002A0C B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002A10 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002A14 AB000DE8           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002A18          
00002A18          
00002A18 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002A1C B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002A20          
00002A20          
00002A20          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A20 E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002A24 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume
00002A28 43A06000           MOVMI   R6,#0                   ;reset to 0 if vol too low
00002A2C E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;store in fake DMA block
00002A30 E0911002           ADDS    R1,R1,R2                ;have we ended (offset becomes GE)?
00002A34 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start
00002A38          
00002A38          
00002A38 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;check fake DMA end...
00002A3C BAFFFFF7           BLT     calcscope_sampleloop    ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002A40 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A44          
00002A44          
00002A44          .calcscope_blankchannel 
00002A44 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;
00002A48          .calcscope_clear ;
00002A48 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A4C E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002A50 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A54 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A58 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A5C B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A60 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A64 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002A68 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002A6C E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit

00003D1C          .stopthesong ;** v1.43 added 32bit compatibility ;)
00003D1C                    OPT opt%
00003D1C E1A0F00E           MOV PC,R14
00003D20                    OPT opt%                       ;no stopping on QTM RasterMan edition v1.45se
00003D20 E13F000F           TEQ     PC,PC                   ;* Test 32bit
00003D28                    FNmrs("EQ","R7,CPSR")           ;*      32bit PSR in R7
00003D28 03878083           ORREQ   R8,R7,#(%1<<7) OR %11   ;*      32bit SVC, no IRQs
00003D30                    FNmsr("EQ","CPSR_c,R8")         ;*      32bit set
00003D30 133FF3C2           TEQNEP  PC,#(%1<<27) OR %11     ;* 26bit SVC mode with ints disabled *not32bit!
00003D34 E1A00000           MOV     R0,R0
00003D38 E92D4000           STMFD   R13!,{R14}
00003D3C EBFFF4F8           BL      swi_qtmpause            ;note - qtmpause must not wait for DMA to clear, or this hangs!
00003D40 E8BD4000           LDMFD   R13!,{R14}
00003D44 E13F000F           TEQ     PC,PC                   ;* Test 32bit
00003D4C                    FNmsr("EQ","CPSR_c,R7")         ;*      32bit restore original mode
00003D4C 133FF382           TEQNEP  PC,#(%1<<27) OR %10     ;* 26bit irq mode, ints off (next must be NOP)
00003D50          
00003D50          
00003D50 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
00003D54 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;set counter to 0 if song stopped, so it can restart instantly
00003D58 E8BD8FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R11,PC}

00003D5C          .set_fineslideup ; ***** E1 - fine slide up *****
00003D5C E59B0020           LDR     R0,[R11,#amigaperiod]
00003D60 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;ensure period>0!
00003D64 08BD80FF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}
00003D68          
00003D68          
00003D68 E5DC7044           LDRB    R7,[R12,#songstatus]    ;v1.44 need to test bit 3, for 3 or 6 octaves
00003D6C E3170008           TST     R7,#%1<<3               ;
00003D70 13A0701C           MOVNE   R7,#28                  ;
00003D74 03A07071           MOVEQ   R7,#113                 ;if 3-octaves, max amiga period is 113, if 6-octaves, max is 28
00003D78          
00003D78          
00003D78 E0400001           SUB     R0,R0,R1
00003D7C E1500007           CMP     R0,R7
00003D80 B1A00007           MOVLT   R0,R7                   ;load period, sub decrement (increase pitch), ensure >= max
00003D84 E58B0020           STR     R0,[R11,#amigaperiod]
00003D88 E58A0008           STR     R0,[R10,#DMA_pitch]
00003D8C E8BD80FF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R7,PC}
00003D90

00003D98 08BD80FF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}

00003DE0          .set_patternloop ; ***** E6 - set pattern loop *****
00003DE0 E3510000           CMP     R1,#0
00003DE4 05DC0010           LDREQB  R0,[R12,#currentevent]
00003DE8 05CB0036           STREQB  R0,[R11,#pattloopstart]
00003DEC 08BD80FF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}         ;if 'set loop', load event and put it in loop start
00003DF0          
00003DF0          
00003DF0 E5DB0037           LDRB    R0,[R11,#pattloopcount]
00003DF4 E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1                ;load current loop value, sub 1
00003DF8 41B00001           MOVMIS  R0,R1                   ; if now <0 then it was 0 (so not in a loop) so set new
00003DFC A5CB0037           STRGEB  R0,[R11,#pattloopcount] ; if new value used or (value-1)>=0, then store new value
00003E00 08BD80FF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}         ; if (value-1)=0 then loop has ended, so exit
00003E04          
00003E04          
00003E04 E5DB0036           LDRB    R0,[R11,#pattloopstart]
00003E08 E5CC001D           STRB    R0,[R12,#pattbreakpos]  ;store loop position in break pos
00003E0C E5DC001C           LDRB    R0,[R12,#breakflags]
00003E10 E3800002           ORR     R0,R0,#%10
00003E14 E5CC001C           STRB    R0,[R12,#breakflags]    ;set break flags
00003E18 E8BD80FF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R7,PC}

00003ED4 A5CB0033           STRGEB  R0,[R11,#retrigtimer]   ;sub 1 from retrig timer, if 0, then reset for next
00003ED8 1AFFFED4           BNE     nextFX

00003F08 EAFFFEC8           B       nextFX

00003F18 E5CB0035           STRB    R0,[R11,#notedeltimer]
00003F1C 1AFFFEC3           BNE     nextFX                  ;if delay >0 then still time to wait, so exit

00003F54          .updatefunk ;R0=invloopspeed, not called if spd=0 (check above and in do_FX)
00003F54 E92D4007           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,R14}
00003F58 E28FE048           ADR     R14,funktable
00003F5C E7DE0000           LDRB    R0,[R14,R0]
00003F60 E5DB103B           LDRB    R1,[R11,#invloopoff]
00003F64 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
00003F68 E5CB103B           STRB    R1,[R11,#invloopoff]
00003F6C E3110080           TST     R1,#%1<<7
00003F70 08BD8007           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R2,PC}
00003F74

00003F74 E3A01000           MOV     R1,#0

00003F7C E59B0000           LDR     R0,[R11,#smp_addr]      ;end address
00003F80 E59B1010           LDR     R1,[R11,#invaddress]
00003F84 E2811001           ADD     R1,R1,#1
00003F88 E1510000           CMP     R1,R0
00003F8C C59B1008           LDRGT   R1,[R11,#smp_replen]
00003F90 C0401001           SUBGT   R1,R0,R1
00003F94 E58B1010           STR     R1,[R11,#invaddress]
00003F98 E5D10000           LDRB    R0,[R1]
00003F9C E2200001           EOR     R0,R0,#1                ;invert bit 0 = change sign!
00003FA0 E5C10000           STRB    R0,[R1]
00003FA4 E8BD8007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,PC}
00003FA8                    ; - this very rare effect inverts actual sample data (in memory),
00003FA8                    ;   and is the only effect NOT included in QTM<v1.48. Although I
00003FA8                    ;   coded the routine, I never found a song which uses it, until
00003FA8                    ;   4mat provided a test track ;) thanks! Now fully implememnted.
00003FA8                    ;                                                             SH
00003FA8          .funktable 
00003FA8 00                 EQUB   0
00003FA9 05                 EQUB   5
00003FAA 06                 EQUB   6
00003FAB 07                 EQUB   7
00003FAC 08                 EQUB   8
00003FAD 0A                 EQUB  10
00003FAE 0B                 EQUB  11
00003FAF 0D                 EQUB  13
00003FB0 10                 EQUB  16
00003FB1 13                 EQUB  19
00003FB2 16                 EQUB  22
00003FB3 1A                 EQUB  26
00003FB4 20                 EQUB  32
00003FB5 2B                 EQUB  43
00003FB6 40                 EQUB  64
00003FB7 80                 EQUB 128
00003FB8                    ALIGN
00003FB8

00003FDC          
00003FDC

00003FE8 E0866202           ADD     R6,R6,R2,LSL#4
00003FEC E59B2020           LDR     R2,[R11,#amigaperiod]
00003FF0          
00003FF0          
00003FF0 E5DC3044           LDRB    R3,[R12,#songstatus]    ;v1.44 need to test bit 3, for 3 or 6 octaves

00003FF8 13A03000           MOVNE   R3,#0                   ;if 6-octaves, use range 0-71
00003FFC 13A05047           MOVNE   R5,#71
00004000 03A0300C           MOVEQ   R3,#12                  ;if 3-octaves, use range 12-47
00004004 03A0502F           MOVEQ   R5,#47

0000402C          .arpeggot 
0000402C E3500001           CMP     R0,#1
00004030 00833221           ADDEQ   R3,R3,R1,LSR#4          ;if counter = 1, then add top 4 bits of data
00004034 B201400F           ANDLT   R4,R1,#&0F
00004038 B0833004           ADDLT   R3,R3,R4                ;if counter < 1 (0), then add bottom 4 bits
0000403C          
0000403C          
0000403C E1B000A3           MOVS    R0,R3,LSR#1
00004040 E7960100           LDR     R0,[R6,R0,LSL#2]        ;load amiga period
00004044 31A00800           MOVCC   R0,R0,LSL#16

000053AC E7D06004           LDRB    R6,[R0,R4]
000053B0 E3560041           CMP     R6,#ASC"A"      ; }
000053B4 A2866020           ADDGE   R6,R6,#32       ; }_ set letters A-Z **ON LY**           *** FAST

0000549C          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
0000559C                    OPT opt%
0000559C          
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
0000559C                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
0000559C                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
0000559C                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
0000559C                    ;
0000559C                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
0000559C                    ;
0000559C          
0000559C          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
0000559C E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
000055A0 E28F2044           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
000055A4          
000055A4                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
000055A4 E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
000055A8 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
000055AC          .shuffleloop 
000055AC E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
000055B0 E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
000055B4 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
000055B8 0A000006           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
000055BC          
000055BC          
000055BC E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
000055C0 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000055C4          
000055C4          
000055C4 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
000055C8 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
000055CC E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
000055D0 E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055D4 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
000055D8          .skipthisshuffle 
000055D8 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
000055DC E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055E0 E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055E4 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055E8 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
000055EC          
000055EC          
000055EC          .shuffletable

0000562C                    ; on entry
0000562C          
0000562C                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
0000562C                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
0000562C                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
0000562C                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
0000562C                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
0000562C                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
0000562C                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
0000562C                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
0000562C                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
0000562C                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
0000562C                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
0000562C                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
0000562C                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
0000562C                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
0000562C                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
0000562C

000067EC                    ; on entry - R0=A +ve; R1=B +ve >0
